import * as React from "react";
import { Navigate, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { useStudentAssignment } from "@/hooks/useStudentAssignments";
import { useUserSettings } from "@/contexts/UserSettingsContext";
import { singleSectionOf } from "@/lib/drillRunSpec";
import { launchDrill } from "@/lib/drillLaunch";
import {
  SECTION_DIRECTIONS,
  formatSectionMinutes,
  sectionMinutes,
} from "@/lib/sectionDirections";
import "./section-directions.css";

/**
 * The screen before an assigned section, laid out the way LawHub lays out its
 * own: a white card on a blue-grey field, the section number, the clock, the
 * question count, the official directions, and one button.
 *
 * Nothing is timed here. The clock starts when GO TO QUESTIONS is pressed,
 * which is also when the assignment becomes "in progress", so a student who
 * opens this and walks away has lost nothing.
 *
 * It takes the whole screen on purpose. The Classroom rail and the list of
 * other work are exactly what a student should not be looking at in the
 * minute before a timed section.
 */
export default function SectionDirectionsPage() {
  const { assignmentId } = useParams<{ assignmentId: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { settings } = useUserSettings();
  const { assignment, loading, error } = useStudentAssignment(assignmentId);

  const section = React.useMemo(
    () => (assignment ? singleSectionOf(assignment.question_qids ?? []) : null),
    [assignment],...
[/external_agent_tool_result]

import * as React from "react";
import { SECTION_DIRECTIONS } from "@/lib/sectionDirections";
import { FileText } from "lucide-react";
import type { HighlightColor } from "@/lib/highlightUtils";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogDescription,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import {
  Popover,
  PopoverContent,
  PopoverTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/popover";
import {
  Tooltip,
  TooltipContent,
  TooltipProvider,
  TooltipTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/tooltip";

type HighlightMode = "none" | "yellow" | "pink" | "orange" | "underline" | "erase";

export const FONT_SCALES = [
  { key: "sm", label: "Small", value: 0.9 },
  { key: "md", label: "Default", value: 1.0 },
  { key: "lg", label: "Large", value: 1.15 },
  { key: "xl", label: "Extra Large", value: 1.3 },
] as const;

export interface ToolbarDirections {
  title: string;
  body: React.ReactNode;
  /** Screen-reader description of the dialog. */
  srDescription: string;
}

/**
 * The official LR section directions. Kept here as the default so the LR
 * player's call sites stay unchanged; RC passes its own set.
 */
export const LR_DIRECTIONS: ToolbarDirections = {
  title: "Directions",
  srDescription: "Directions for the LSAT Logical Reasoning section.",
  // LSAC's current digital wording, the same text the section's directions
  // page shows. The old paper-test version told students to blacken a space
  // on an answer sheet they do not have.
  body: <p>{SECTION_DIRECTIONS.lr}</p>,
};

interface FontScaleOption {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  value: number;
}

interface Props {
  highlightMode: HighlightMode;
  setHighlightMode: (mode: HighlightMode) => void;
  /** Pre-formatted timer label, e.g. "20:41". Empty string when no timer. */
  timerLabel: string;
  /** Actual timer mode — drives label prefix exactly. Undefined hides timer box. */
  timerMode?: "countdown" | "stopwatch";
  /** Paint the timer as overrun. A countdown that has passed zero keeps going. */
  timerOverBudget?: boolean;
  /**
   * Fraction of the section's time still left, 0..1. Drives the depleting bar
   * the official interface paints under the clock. Omit for a stopwatch or an
   * untimed section and the bar is not rendered — there is nothing to deplete.
   */
  timerProgress?: number;
  /** Controlled Find Text value. */
  searchTerm: string;
  setSearchTerm: (s: string) => void;
  /** Active font scale value (1.0 = default). */
  fontScale: number;
  setFontScale: (n: number) => void;
  /** Show the notes toggle icon (Smart Drill only). */
  notesButtonVisible?: boolean;
  /** Whether the notes panel is currently open (drives the active state). */
  notesOpen?: boolean;
  onToggleNotes?: () => void;
  /**
   * Directions dialog contents. Defaults to the LR section directions; pass a
   * different set for another section, or `null` to hide the button entirely.
   */
  directions?: ToolbarDirections | null;
  /**
   * Rendered in the left group between Directions and Find Text — the slot RC
   * uses for "Passage Only View", which sits there in the official interface.
   */
  leftTools?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Rendered in the right cluster after the text controls and before the timer.
   * RC puts its find-match readout and the next/previous match buttons here.
   * (This used to claim response masking, a review grid and full screen, none
   * of which RC has ever passed.)
   */
  rightTools?: React.ReactNode;
  /**
   * Hide the underline/marker/eraser group. Only pass false for a surface that
   * has no text to annotate — the tools are part of the official look.
   */
  highlightToolsVisible?: boolean;
  /** Override the font-size options. Defaults to FONT_SCALES. */
  fontScaleOptions?: readonly FontScaleOption[];
  /**
   * Extra controls appended inside the Aa popover beneath a divider. RC puts
   * line height and color scheme here so text settings stay in one control.
   */
  a11yExtraSection...

  };

  const timerLabelPrefix =
    timerMode === "countdown"
      ? "Section Time Remaining"
      : timerMode === "stopwatch"
      ? "Time Elapsed"
      : null;
  const paddedTimer = (timerLabel || "00:00").replace(/^(\d):/, "0$1:");
  // The bar is the at-a-glance read; the digits are the precise one. It goes
  // amber in the last quarter and red in the last tenth so the student feels
  // the section closing without having to parse numbers mid-question.
  const timerFraction =
    typeof timerProgress === "number"
      ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, timerProgress))
      : null;
  const timerBarColor =
    timerOverBudget || (timerFraction !== null && timerFraction <= 0.1)
      ? "bg-red-600"
      : timerFraction !== null && timerFraction <= 0.25
      ? "bg-amber-500"
      : "bg-green-600";

  return (
    <>
      <div className="border-b border-slate-300 bg-slate-50">
        <div className="px-3 sm:px-4 py-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-[14px] text-slate-800">
          {sectionToggle && (
            <div
              role="group"
              aria-label="Section"
              className="mr-1 inline-flex overflow-hidden rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white"
            >
              {(["lr", "rc"] as const).map((section) => {
                const isActive = sectionToggle.active === section;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={section}
                    type="button"
                    aria-pressed={isActive}
                    title={section === "lr" ? "Logical Reasoning" : "Reading Comprehension passages"}
                    onClick={() => !isActive && sectionToggle.onSelect(section)}
                    className={cn(
                      "px-3 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider transition-colors",
                      isActive
                        ? "bg-[#7f1d1d] text-white"
                        : "text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100",
                    )}
                  >
                    {section.toUpperCase()}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}

          {directions && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setDirectionsOpen(true)}
              className="rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors text-[14px] font-semibold"
            >
              Directions
            </button>
          )}

          {leftTools}

          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Find Text, Type Here"
            aria-label="Find text in passage"
            className="ml-1 rounded-sm border border-slate-500 bg-white px-3 h-8 text-[13px] text-slate-800 placeholder:text-slate-400 w-[210px] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-slate-700"
          />
          {searchTerm && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSearchTerm("")}
              className="text-[12px] text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 underline"
              aria-label="Clear search"
            >
              clear
            </button>
          )}

          {/* Right cluster: tools | Aa + line-spacing | timer */}
          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5">
            {highlightToolsVisible && (
              <>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-0.5">
                  {tools.map((t) => {
                    const isActive = highlightMode === t.mode;
                    return (
                      <button

command: cd "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-fix-homework-timed-launch" && cat >> src/pages/section-directions.css <<'EOF'

/*
 * The same screen mid-section, inside the player's white card. The clock is
 * already running, so there is no back link and no full-viewport height: the
 * field fills whatever the player gives it and scrolls on its own.
 */
.section-directions-view {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif;
  color: #1f1f1f;
}

.section-directions-bar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 12px 20px 16px;
  background: #ffffff;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #d5dce3;
}

.section-directions-questions {
  background: #5f6368;
  color: #ffffff;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 4px;
  padding: 8px 16px;
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 700;
  cursor: pointer;
}

.section-directions-questions:hover,
.section-directions-questions:focus-visible {
  background: #4a4e52;
  outline: 3px solid #1f1f1f;
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

.section-directions-clock {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: flex-end;
  line-height: 1.3;
}

.section-directions-clock-label {
  font-size: 13px;
  letter-spacing: 0.06em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #5f6368;
}

.section-directions-clock-time {
  font-size: 19px;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.section-directions-clock-time--over {
  color: #b91c1c;
}

.section-directions--embedded {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 48px 24px 40px;
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .section-directions--embedded {
    padding: 24px 12px 32px;
  }
}
EOF

                // An accommodated student carries her clock on the launch,
                // the same way a section sat from the section door does.
                mode: launch.timerMode ?? '35',
                isPaused: false,
                elapsedMs: 0,
                startedAt: now,
              },
            },
            // A section assigned as a measurement stays sealed until it is
            // scored. Blind review is where the thinking gets checked.
            sealed: !launch.revealAllowed,
            assignmentId: launch.assignmentId,
            guidedSetId: launch.guidedSetId,
          };
        }
      }

      const spec: DrillRunSpec = {
        ...base,
        mode: 'type-drill',
        config: typeDrillConfig(resolvedQids),
        sealed: false,
        isGuidedDrill: launch.kind === 'guided-drill',
      };
      if (launch.kind === 'assignment') {
        spec.assignmentId = launch.assignmentId;
        spec.guidedSetId = launch.guidedSetId;
      }
      if (launch.kind === 'guided-drill') {
        spec.guidedDurationMinutes = launch.durationMinutes;
        spec.guidedDrillId = launch.drillId;
        spec.guidedAssignmentId = launch.assignmentId;
      }
      if (launch.kind === 'practice-set') {
        // The two values the runner uses to recognise an Intro to LR practice
        // set and map it back to its module-progress completion id.
        spec.practiceSource = launch.source ?? 'intro-to-lr-practice-drills';
        spec.practiceSlug = launch.slug;
      }
[/external_agent_tool_result]

[external_agent_tool_result]
export function launchDrill(launch: DrillLaunch): string {
  const p = new URLSearchParams();
  p.set('drill', launch.kind);

  switch (launch.kind) {
    case 'smart-drill':
      break;
    case 'section':
      p.set('pt', String(launch.pt));
      p.set('section', String(launch.section));
      if (launch.timerMode) p.set('timer', launch.timerMode);
      if (launch.minutes) p.set('minutes', String(launch.minutes));
      if (launch.sealed) p.set('sealed', '1');
      if (launch.ptLeg) {
        p.set('runId', launch.ptLeg.runId);
        p.set('leg', String(launch.ptLeg.legIndex));
      }
      break;
    case 'saved-set':
      p.set('set', launch.setId);
      break;
    case 'assignment':
      p.set('assignment', launch.assignmentId);
      if (launch.guidedSetId) p.set('guidedSet', launch.guidedSetId);
[/external_agent_tool_result]

  // unless the student ticks "Don't show me this message again", which holds

import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";

  questionCount: 26,

};

describe("SectionDirectionsView", () => {
  it("shows the section card with the running clock, padded like the toolbar", () => {

  it("returns to the questions from either button", () => {

    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "GO TO QUESTIONS" }));

  });

    );

    expect(screen.getByText("Untimed section")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

Done. Directions inside a section now behaves like your screenshot, and it's pushed to PR #121 as commit 5 (`615500b`).

**What changed**

- Pressing **Directions** in a full section swaps the player for the directions screen: `Questions` button top-left, `TIME REMAINING` clock top-right, the white card on the blue-grey field, `GO TO QUESTIONS` underneath. Both buttons return to the same question you left.
- The timer is never touched. Verified on the dev build: 34:56 on the question → 34:48 on directions → 34:17 back on Question 1 → 33:42 on directions again, one continuous countdown.
- The card is now one shared component ([SectionDirectionsCard.tsx](src/components/drill/official/SectionDirectionsCard.tsx)) used by both the pre-section page and the in-section view, so they can't drift. Title is now the big bold "Section 2" as in LawHub, on both.
- Non-section drills (smart drill, type drill) keep the old Directions dialog; only full sections and PT sittings get the takeover.
- 3 new tests; the 8 surrounding suites pass (50 tests); typecheck clean.

**Not exercised live:** Time's up firing while the directions view is open (it's a dialog outside the player, so it should still appear, but I didn't sit out 35 minutes to prove it).

export type HighlightColor = 'yellow' | 'pink' | 'orange' | 'underline';

export interface Highlight {
  id: string;
  start: number;
  end: number;
  text: string;
  color: HighlightColor;
  section: 'stimulus' | 'stem';
}

export function captureTextSelection(container: HTMLElement): { start: number; end: number; text: string } | null {
  const selection = window.getSelection();
  if (!selection || selection.rangeCount === 0 || selection.isCollapsed) return null;

  const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
  if (!container.contains(range.commonAncestorContainer)) return null;

  const selectedText = selection.toString();
  if (!selectedText) return null;

  // ── Accurate offset mapping via data-para-start annotations ──────────────
  // HighlightedText annotates each <p> / dialogue-turn body with its raw-string
  // start offset. Within a single annotated segment there are no block boundaries,
  // so DOM text length == raw text length — no \n\n drift.
  const segments = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-para-start]'));

  if (segments.length > 0) {
    // Returns the character offset from the start of `seg` to `node:nodeOffset`
    const withinOffset = (seg: HTMLElement, node: Node, nodeOffset: number): number => {
      const r = document.createRange();
      r.setStart(seg, 0);
      r.setEnd(node, nodeOffset);
      return r.toString().length;
    };

    // Find the annotated segment that contains a given DOM node
    const findSeg = (node: Node): HTMLElement | null => {
      for (const seg of segments) {
        if (seg === node || seg.contains(node)) return seg;
      }
      return null;
    };

    const startSeg = findSeg(range.startContainer);
    const endSeg   = findSeg(range.endContainer);

    if (startSeg && endSeg) {
      const rawStart = parseInt(startSeg.getAttribute('data-para-start') ?? '0')
        + withinOffset(startSeg, range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
      const rawEnd   = parseInt(endSeg.getAttribute('data-para-start') ?? '0')
        + withinOffset(endSeg, range.endContainer, range.endOffset);
      return { start: rawStart, end: rawEnd, text: selectedText };
    }
  }

  // ── Fallback for containers without data-para-start ──────────────────────
  const containerRange = document.createRange();
  containerRange.selectNodeContents(container);
  const beforeRange = containerRange.cloneRange();
  beforeRange.setEnd(range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
  const start = beforeRange.toString().length;
  const end   = start + selectedText.length;
  return { start, end, text: selectedText };
}

export function replaceOverlappingHighlights(
  existing: Highlight[], 
  newHighlight: Highlight
): Highlight[] {
  const result: Highlight[] = [];
  
  for (const h of existing) {
    // No overlap - keep as is
    if (h.end <= newHighlight.start || h.start >= newHighlight.end) {
      result.push(h);
      continue;
    }
    
    // Has overlap - split and keep non-overlapping portions
    // Keep the part before the new highlight
    if (h.start < newHighlight.start) {
      const beforeText = h.text.slice(0, newHighlight.start - h.start);
      result.push({
        ...h,
        id: `${h.id}-before`,
        end: newHighlight.start,
        text: beforeText
      });
    }
    
    // Keep the part after the new highlight
    if (h.end > newHighlight.end) {
      const offset = newHighlight.end - h.start;
      const afterText = h.text.slice(offset);
      result.push({
        ...h,
        id: `${h.id}-after`,
        start: newHighlight.end,
        text: afterText
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Add the new highlight
  result.push(newHighlight);
  
  // Merge adjacent highlights of the same color
  return mergeAdjacentSameColor(result);
}

function mergeAdjacentSameColor(highlights: Highlight[]): Highlight[] {
  if (highlights.length <= 1) return highlights;

  const handleTextSelection = (e: React.MouseEvent, section: 'stimulus' | 'stem') => {
    if (highlightMode === 'none') return;
    
    const container = e.currentTarget as HTMLElement;
    const selection = captureTextSelection(container);
    
    if (!selection || !currentQuestion) return;
    
    const currentHighlights = highlights.get(currentQuestion.qid) || [];
    
    // Save to history before making changes
    const MAX_HISTORY = 20;
    setHighlightHistory(prev => {
      const newHistory = [...prev, new Map(highlights)];
      return newHistory.slice(-MAX_HISTORY);
    });
    
    if (highlightMode === 'erase') {
      // Eraser mode: remove overlapping portions
      const updatedHighlights = currentHighlights.filter(h => {
        // Keep highlights from different sections
        if (h.section !== section) return true;
        
        // Check for overlap
        return h.end <= selection.start || h.start >= selection.end;
      }).concat(
        // Split and keep non-overlapping portions
        currentHighlights
          .filter(h => h.section === section && h.start < selection.end && h.end > selection.start)
          .flatMap(h => {
            const parts: Highlight[] = [];
            
            // Keep the part before the erased selection
            if (h.start < selection.start) {
              parts.push({

                end: selection.start,
                text: h.text.slice(0, selection.start - h.start)

            // Keep the part after the erased selection
            if (h.end > selection.end) {
              parts.push({
                ...h,
                id: `${h.id}-after`,
                start: selection.end,
                text: h.text.slice(selection.end - h.start)
              });
            }
            
            return parts;
          })
      );
      
      setHighlights(new Map(highlights.set(currentQuestion.qid, updatedHighlights)));

      <span
        key={`${prefix}-h${h.id}`}
        data-highlight-id={h.id}
        className={cn('hl', 'transition-all', eraserMode && 'cursor-pointer hover:opacity-60')}
        style={getHighlightStyles(h.color)}
        onClick={() => eraserMode && onHighlightClick?.(h.id)}

      </span>
    );
    cursor = h.end;
  }

  if (cursor < text.length) {
    const slice = text.slice(cursor);
    nodes.push(<span key={`${prefix}-tend`}>{renderWithSearch(slice, searchTerm, `${prefix}-tend`)}</span>);
  }

  return <>{nodes}</>;
}

// ─── Component ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export function HighlightedText({
  text,
  highlights,
  onHighlightClick,
  eraserMode,
  searchTerm,
}: HighlightedTextProps) {
  const speakerPattern = /^([A-Z][A-Za-z’' -]{1,80}):\s+/;
  const turnSplitPattern = /(?=^[A-Z][A-Za-z’' -]{1,80}:\s)/gm;
  const rawSegments = text.split(turnSplitPattern).filter(s => s.trim());

  const dialogueTurns = rawSegments
    .map(seg => {
      const match = seg.match(speakerPattern);
      if (match && looksLikeSpeakerLabel(match[1])) {
        return { speaker: match[1], text: seg.slice(match[0].length).trim() };
      }
      return null;
    })
    .filter(Boolean);

  const isDialogue =
    dialogueTurns.length >= 2 &&
    dialogueTurns.length === rawSegments.length &&
    dialogueTurns.every(t => t !== null);

  if (isDialogue) {
    let currentOffset = 0;
    const turnRanges = dialogueTurns.map(turn => {
      const speakerPrefix = `${turn!.speaker}: `;
      const turnStart = text.indexOf(speakerPrefix, currentOffset);
      const start = turnStart + speakerPrefix.length;
      const end = start + turn!.text.length;
      currentOffset = end;
      return { turn: turn!, start, end };
    });

    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {turnRanges.map((tr, idx) => {
          const turnHighlights = highlights
            .filter(h => h.start < tr.end && h.end > tr.start)
            .map(h => ({
              ...h,
              start: Math.max(0, h.start - tr.start),
              end: Math.min(tr.turn.text.length, h.end - tr.start),
            }));

          return (
            <div key={idx} className="leading-relaxed" style={{ lineHeight: 1.7 }}>
              <span className="font-bold">{tr.turn.speaker}:</span>{' '}
              <span className="inline" data-para-start={tr.start}>
                {renderSegments(tr.turn.text, turnHighlights, `d${idx}`, eraserMode, onHighlightClick, searchTerm)}
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const paragraphs = text.split('\n\n');

  let currentOffset = 0;
  const paragraphRanges = paragraphs.map(para => {
    const start = currentOffset;
    const end = currentOffset + para.length;
    currentOffset = end + 2;
    return { start, end, text: para };
  });

  return (
    <>
      {paragraphRanges.map((pr, idx) => {
        const paraHighlights = highlights
          .filter(h => h.start < pr.end && h.end > pr.start)
          .map(h => ({
            ...h,
            start: Math.max(0, h.start - pr.start),
            end: Math.min(pr.text.length, h.end - pr.start),
          }));

        return (
          <p key={idx} data-para-start={pr.start} style={{ margin: '0 0 12px', lineHeight: 1.6 }}>
            {renderSegments(pr.text, paraHighlights, `p${idx}`, eraserMode, onHighlightClick, searchTerm)}
          </p>
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
}

No file under src/features/activity-centre/ renders questionStem or answerChoices; src/components/drill/Guided* (GuidedDrillLaunchFrame, GuidedDrillIntroCard, GuidedDrillIntroModal, GuidedDrillCardPreview) and src/pages/GuidedDrillStart.tsx contain no questionStem/answerChoices/OfficialQuestionPane references (grep returned nothing); guided drills run inside src/pages/Drill.tsx (isGuidedDrillRunner L3772) and therefore inherit the primary surface's fix.",

      "sharedCore": "Three shared changes, and they are the whole fix; the per-surface work above is wiring. (1) src/lib/highlightUtils.ts L9: `section: 'stimulus' | 'stem'` has no room for a choice. Extend to `'stimulus' | 'stem' | 'choice'` plus an optional `choiceKey?: string` (or encode as `choice:A`), because each choice is its own raw string with its own 0-based offset space. (2) replaceOverlappingHighlights (L67-110) compares offsets across the whole array with no section check at L75, so a stem highlight at 0-20 currently splits or removes a stimulus highlight at 0-20 for the same question; only the adjacent-merge at L123 checks section. Both callers pass the full per-qid array (BlindReviewFlow L133, Drill.tsx L2781). Either filter by section (and choiceKey) inside the function or have every caller partition before calling; the Drill.tsx eraser branch (L2729-2768) already does this by hand and shows the intended shape. (3) src/components/drill/official/OfficialQuestionPane.tsx: stem selection is already wired (onMouseUp L166, HighlightedText L172-178, stemHighlights L88), but choices are rendered as `<button>` text at L253-270, and browsers do not start a text selection from a drag inside a button, which is why nothing can be highlighted there on any surface that uses the pane. Render each choice's text through HighlightedText (with `highlights.filter(h => h.section === 'choice' && h.choiceKey === key)`, eraserMode, searchTerm) inside a non-button element that carries onMouseUp={(e) => onTextSelection(e, 'choice', key)} and toggles select-text when highlightMode !== 'none', while keeping the click-to-select behaviour on the badge button (L235-252) and via an onClick on the text wrapper that ignores non-collapsed selections. HighlightedText already emits data-para-start on each <p> (L225), so captureTextSelection works unchanged as long as the choice text is the only text inside the onMouseUp container (the letter badge must stay outside it or the fallback path at highlightUtils L57-64 mis-offsets). The `onTextSelection` prop type at L40 becomes `(e, section: 'stimulus' | 'stem' | 'choice', choiceKey?: string) => void`; with `strict: false` (tsconfig.app.json L25) the existing no-op and Drill.tsx handlers k...

Across the non-primary LR surfaces, only BlindReviewFlow (the legacy, effectively unreachable blind review) has working stem highlighting; nothing anywhere highlights answer choices. The two surfaces students actually drill in outside src/pages/Drill.tsx both reuse OfficialQuestionPane but neuter it: EnhancedBlindReview shows the full highlight toolbar yet passes onTextSelection={() => {}} and onHighlightClick={() => {}} (L334-335) with no selection handler at all, so stimulus, stem and choices are all inert there; LRQuestionModal (bootcamp Capstone / Hard Sentences Capstone) passes highlights={[]} highlightMode="none" and no-op handlers (L312-318) and has no toolbar. SectionReviewWalk is read-only by design and passes noop (L206-207). CSQuestionCard (Causation Station) is a separate one-colour {start,end} model on the stimulus only (L93-155, L240-246) and does not use the shared core. ClassroomQuestionPane, JournalQuestionPane, JournalQuestionView, QuestionPicker, FlaggedQuestionsList and TutorChatModal render or forward question text but are review/lesson/admin surfaces with no highlight tooling and are out of scope. Nothing under src/features/activity-centre/ renders an LR stem + choices (SectionStage is stimulus-only, SlugJudge is a game wing), and the src/components/drill/Guided* files are launch/preview chrome that never render a question. The real fix is in the shared core: OfficialQuestionPane already wires stem selection (L164-179) but renders each choice's text inside a <button> (L253-270), which browsers do not let the user drag-select, and the Highlight model has no choice section (highlightUtils.ts L9); once the pane supports choices, EnhancedBlindReview needs a real handler and LRQuestionModal needs state + toolbar. Four call sites pass onTextSelection/onHighlightClick into the pane and would need matching edits if its prop signature changes: Drill.tsx L4290-4291, EnhancedBlindReview L334-335, SectionReviewWalk L206-207, LRQuestionModal L317-318 (plus the test at OfficialQuestionPane.test.tsx L33-34, L100-101).

import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import OfficialQuestionPane from "./OfficialQuestionPane";

const question = {
  qid: "q-26",
  qnum: 26,
  stimulus:
    "A recent poll showed that almost half of the city's residents believe that Mayor Walker is guilty of ethics violations.",
  questionStem:
    "Which one of the following, if true, most helps to explain the surprising fact stated above?",
  answerChoices: {
    A: "Almost all of the people who believe that Walker is guilty of ethics violations had thought his performance was poor.",
    B: "There has been an increase in ratings for Walker's political opponents.",
  },
};

function renderPane(questionOverride = question) {
  return render(
    <OfficialQuestionPane
      question={questionOverride}
      displayNumber={26}
      selectedAnswer="A"
      eliminatedAnswers={new Set()}
      isFlagged={false}
      answerRevealed
      correctAnswer="A"
      highlights={[]}
      highlightMode="none"
      onAnswerSelect={vi.fn()}
      onEliminate={vi.fn()}
      onToggleFlag={vi.fn()}
      onTextSelection={vi.fn()}
      onHighlightClick={vi.fn()}
    />,
  );
}

  });

    const { container } = renderPane({
      ...question,
      stimulus:
        "Figorian Wildlife Commission: First speaker text.\n\nFigorian Development Commission: Second speaker text.",
    });

export default function OfficialQuestionPane({
  question,
  displayNumber,
  selectedAnswer,
  eliminatedAnswers,
  isFlagged,
  answerRevealed = false,
  correctAnswer,
  inlineNotes,
  highlights,
  highlightMode,
  searchTerm,
  fontScale = 1,
  onAnswerSelect,
  onEliminate,
  onToggleFlag,
  onTextSelection,
  onHighlightClick,
  aboveChoices,
}: Props) {
  const stimulusHighlights = highlights.filter((h) => h.section === "stimulus");
  const stemHighlights = highlights.filter((h) => h.section === "stem");
  const stimulusText = stripDataArtifacts(question.stimulus);
  const stemText = stripDataArtifacts(question.questionStem);
  const answerEntries = Object.entries(question.answerChoices).map(([key, text]) => [
    key,
    cleanAnswerChoice(key, text),
  ] as const);

  // 7Sage sets both the stimulus and the answer choices at 19px and the
  // student reads ours next to theirs, so the two match rather than land a
  // couple of pixels apart. The stimulus carries the extra weight of the serif
  // face; stem and choices stay on the sans stack at the same size.
  const baseSize = 19 * fontScale;
  const officialContentTextStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    color: "#111827",
    fontFamily: '"Helvetica Neue", Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    fontSize: `${baseSize}px`,
    fontWeight: 400,
    lineHeight: 1.6,
  };
  const stimStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    ...officialContentTextStyle,
    fontFamily: "Fraunces, ui-serif, Georgia, serif",
    fontSize: `${19 * fontScale}px`,
    lineHeight: 1.7,
    // Fraunces is a variable font with an optical-size axis. Left on `auto` the
    // browser picks the cut from the rendered px size, which drifts as the
    // student changes fontScale. Pin it to the text-optimised end so hairlines
    // stay the same weight at every scale.
    fontOpticalSizing: "none",
    fontVariationSettings: '"opsz" 14',
  };
  const stemStyle: React.CSSProperties = officialContentTextStyle;
  // The answer-choice text and letter badges used Tailwind's `text-sm`/`text-xs`,
  // which are hard px values and silently overrode the inherited scaled size —
  // so fontScale grew the stimulus and stem while the answers stayed at 14px.
  const choiceTextStyle: React.CSSProperties = { fontSize: `${baseSize}px` };
  const choiceBadgeStyle: React.CSSProperties = { fontSize: `${baseSize * 0.78}px` };

  return (
    <div className="relative flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 bg-white text-slate-950">
      <DrillSplit
        ratio="passage-weighted"
        maxWidthClassName={null}
        left={
          // Stimulus column
          <div
            className={cn(
              "min-w-0 px-6 pt-6 pb-7 sm:pl-9 sm:pr-8 lg:border-r border-slate-200 whitespace-pre-wrap overflow-y-auto",
              // Cap the reading measure. The frame is max-w-[1400px] and this column
              // takes 53fr of it, which ran ~100 characters per line — well past the
              // 45-75 readable band. `ch` is the width of "0", which is narrow in
              // Fraunces, so 52ch measures out to ~68 real characters.
[/external_agent_tool_result]

/**
 * LRQuestionModal — shared LR Smart Drill–styled modal for any LSAT-format
 * multiple-choice question across the app.
 *
 * Two modes:
 *   1. Bank-backed: pass { initialQid, queue } — pulls from questionBank,
 *      logs an `attempts` row on submit (mode = `attemptMode`, default
 *      "bootcamp-pf").
 *   2. Inline: pass { inline: { ...item } } — used for bootcamp content
 *      that lives outside the LSAT U bank (e.g. Capstone, Simulator).
 *      Calls `onSubmit({ key, correct })` so the host can record progress.
 */

import * as React from "react";
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { X, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, CheckCircle2, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";

  type InlineChoiceNotes,
} from "@/components/drill/official/InlineChoiceExplanation";

import { useAuth } from "@/contexts/AuthContext";
import { useClassId } from "@/hooks/useClassId";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";

export interface InlineQuestionInput {
  /** Stable id for this item (used in header + persistence). */
  id: string;
  /** Optional display number (defaults to 1). */
  number?: number | string;
  stimulus: string;
  questionStem: string;
  /** Map of letter → choice text. Any A..E subset works. */
  answerChoices: Record<string, string>;
  correctAnswer: string;
  /** Optional per-choice explanation for the post-submit panel. */
  explanations?: Partial<

  // Bank-backed nav state
  const queue = props.queue ?? [];
  const initialQid = props.initialQid ?? "";
  const startIdx = Math.max(0, queue.indexOf(initialQid));
  const [idx, setIdx] = React.useState(startIdx);
  const qid = isInline ? props.inline!.id : queue[idx] ?? initialQid;

  const [bankQuestion, setBankQuestion] = React.useState<LRQuestion | undefined>(undefined);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!isInline) setBankQuestion(questionBank.getQuestion(qid));
  }, [qid, qbLoading, isInline]);

  const inline = isInline ? props.inline! : null;

  // Normalized question for OfficialQuestionPane
  const renderQuestion = React.useMemo(() => {
    if (inline) {
      return {
        qid: inline.id,
        qnum: inline.number,
        stimulus: inline.stimulus || "",
        questionStem: inline.questionStem || "",
        answerChoices: inline.answerChoices,
      };
    }
    if (!bankQuestion) return null;
    return {
      qid: bankQuestion.qid,
      qnum: bankQuestion.qnum,
      stimulus: bankQuestion.stimulus || "",
      questionStem: bankQuestion.questionStem,
      answerChoices: bankQuestion.answerChoices,
    };
  }, [inline, bankQuestion]);

  const correctAnswer = inline?.correctAnswer ?? bankQuestion?.correctAnswer ?? "";
  const explanations = inline?.explanations ?? bankQuestion?.answerChoiceExplanations;

  const [selected, setSelected] = React.useState("");
  const [eliminated, setEliminated] = React.useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = React.useState(false);
  const startTsRef = React.useRef<number>(Date.now());

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setSelected("");
    setEliminated(new Set());
    setSubmitted(false);
    startTsRef.current = Date.now();
  }, [qid]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === "Escape") props.onClose(); };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [props.onClose]);

  it("marks the choice they actually took, not only the one that was right", () => {
    renderRevealed();

    // B was chosen and is wrong; saying "A is correct" without saying "and you
    // took B" is only half of what a reveal is for.
    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: /There has been an increase in ratings/i,
      }),
    ).toHaveClass("text-red-900");
  });

  it("does not leave the verdict to colour alone", () => {
    // ADR 0001: feedback must always carry an icon and a label, so a student who
    // cannot separate the green from the red still gets the answer.
    renderRevealed();

    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Correct answer")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Your answer, incorrect")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("shows the answer without unfolding every explanation", () => {
    // Answer and Explanation are separate asks. Revealing with no inline notes
    // passed must colour the choices and add nothing else.
    renderRevealed({ inlineNotes: null });

    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Correct answer")).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/why this is/i)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("unfolds them when the notes are handed over, which is Explanation's job", () => {
    // The other half of the pair: without this, the assertion above would pass
    // just as happily if the inline layer had been deleted outright.
    renderRevealed({
      inlineNotes: {
        A: { tag: null, studentPercent: null, note: "A is the only choice that explains it." },
        B: { tag: null, studentPercent: null, note: "B changes the subject." },
      },
    });

    expect(screen.getByText(/why this is correct/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/why this is wrong/i)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it("leaves an untaken wrong choice alone", () => {
    // Only the choice they committed to turns red. The other wrong answers are
    // not accusations.
    renderRevealed({ selectedAnswer: "A" });

    expect(
      screen.getByRole("button", {
        name: /There has been an increase in ratings/i,
      }),
    ).not.toHaveClass("text-red-900");
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("Your answer, incorrect")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import OfficialQuestionPane from "./OfficialQuestionPane";
import { choiceSection, type Highlight } from "@/lib/highlightUtils";

/** The element that carries a choice's colour classes: the text block itself. */
function choiceText(name: RegExp): HTMLElement {
  const el = screen.getByText(name).closest("[id^='choice-']");
  if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) throw new Error(`no choice block for ${name}`);
  return el;
}

/** Fake a live selection of `text` so the pane sees a drag, not a click. */
function selectText(text: string) {
  const node = screen.getByText(text).firstChild as Text;
  const range = document.createRange();
  range.setStart(node, 0);
  range.setEnd(node, text.length);
  const selection = window.getSelection()!;
  selection.removeAllRanges();
  selection.addRange(range);
}

afterEach(() => {
  window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges();
});

describe("OfficialQuestionPane answer choices as highlight surfaces", () => {
  const stemRegionCount = 2; // stimulus + stem

  function renderLive(props: Partial<React.ComponentProps<typeof OfficialQuestionPane>> = {}) {
    const onAnswerSelect = vi.fn();
    const onTextSelection = vi.fn();
    render(
      <OfficialQuestionPane
        question={question}
        displayNumber={26}
        selectedAnswer=""
        eliminatedAnswers={new Set()}
        isFlagged={false}
        highlights={[]}
        highlightMode="none"
        onAnswerSelect={onAnswerSelect}
        onEliminate={vi.fn()}
        onToggleFlag={vi.fn()}
        onTextSelection={onTextSelection}
        onHighlightClick={vi.fn()}
        {...props}
      />,
    );
    return { onAnswerSelect, onTextSelection };
  }

  it("hands every block to the selection handler, choices included", () => {
    const { onTextSelection } = renderLive();
    fireEvent.mouseUp(screen.getByText(question.answerChoices.B));
    const regions = onTextSelection.mock.calls[0][0];
    expect(regions.map((r: { section: string }) => r.section)).toEqual([
      "stimulus",
      "stem",
      choiceSection("A"),
      choiceSection("B"),
    ]);
    expect(regions).toHaveLength(stemRegionCount + 2);
    // Each region's source is the text the offsets will be measured against.
    expect(regions[3].source).toBe(question.answerChoices.B);
    expect(regions[3].container).toBe(choiceText(/There has been an increase/));
  });

  it("still picks the choice on a plain click", () => {
    const { onAnswerSelect } = renderLive();
    const text = screen.getByText(question.answerChoices.B);
    fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
    fireEvent.click(text);
    expect(onAnswerSelect).toHaveBeenCalledWith("B");
  });

  it("does not pick the choice when the mouse-up ends a drag", () => {
    const { onAnswerSelect } = renderLive({ highlightMode: "yellow" });
    const text = screen.getByText(question.answerChoices.B);
    selectText(question.answerChoices.B);
    fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
    fireEvent.click(text);
    expect(onAnswerSelect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("does not pick the choice when the handler consumed the selection", () => {
    // The handler clears the selection once it has made a highlight, so by
    // click time the selection is collapsed; the handler's return value is
    // what tells the click apart from a plain click.
    const { onAnswerSelect } = renderLive({
      highlightMode: "yellow",
      onTextSelection: () => {
        window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges();
        return true;
      },
    });
    const text = screen.getByText(question.answerChoices.B);
    selectText(question.answerChoices.B);
    fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
    fireEvent.click(text);
    expect(onAnswerSelect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("does not pick a choice from a click in eraser mode", () => {
    const { onAnswerSelect } = renderLive({ highlightMode: "erase" });
    const text = screen.getByText(question.answerChoices.B);
    fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
    fireEvent.click(text);
    expect(onAnswerSelect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("renders a choice's highlight inside that choice only", () => {
    const highlights: Highlight[] = [
      { id: "h1", start: 0, end: 5, text: "There", color: "yellow", section: choiceSection("B") },
      { id: "h2", start: 0, end: 6, text: "Almost", color: "pink", section: choiceSection("A") },
    ];
    renderLive({ highlights });
    const b = choiceText(/There has been an increase/);
    const a = choiceText(/Almost all of the people/);
    expect(b.querySelector("[data-highlight-id='h1']")).toHaveTextContent("There");
    expect(b.querySelector("[data-highlight-id='h2']")).toBeNull();
    expect(a.querySelector("[data-highlight-id='h2']")).toHaveTextContent("Almost");
  });

  it("keeps the letter badge as the keyboard control, described by its text", () => {
    const { onAnswerSelect } = renderLive();
    const badge = screen.getByRole("button", { name: "B" });
    expect(badge).toHaveAttribute("aria-describedby", `choice-${question.qid}-B`);
    fireEvent.click(badge);
    expect(onAnswerSelect).toHaveBeenCalledWith("B");
  });
});
'''

## assistant

[external_agent_tool_call: Bash]
description: Commit the LR highlight work
command: cd "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-fix-homework-timed-launch" && git status --short && git add src/lib/highlightUtils.ts src/lib/highlightUtils.test.ts src/hooks/useQuestionHighlights.ts src/hooks/useQuestionHighlights.test.ts src/components/drill/HighlightedText.tsx src/components/drill/official/OfficialQuestionPane.tsx src/components/drill/official/OfficialQuestionPane.test.tsx src/components/drill/EnhancedBlindReview.tsx src/components/drill/SectionReviewWalk.tsx src/components/drill/shared/LRQuestionModal.tsx src/pages/Drill.tsx && git commit -q -m "Highlight and underline the stem and every answer choice, on every LR surface

Answer choices were rendered as buttons, and a browser will not start a
drag-selection inside a button, so the marker tools only ever reached the
stimulus. The choice text is now a plain block that still picks the
choice on a click and never on a drag; the letter badge stays the
keyboard control.

A stem highlight also used to vanish the moment the stimulus was
highlighted: overlap was tested by offset alone, with no section check,
so offset 0 of the stem and offset 0 of the stimulus fought each other.
Every section, each choice included, now has its own lane.

One drag can cross the stem and two choices; each block gets its own
mark. A selection that starts on the question number snaps to the start
of the stem instead of shifting the mark by four characters.

Highlight state moves into useQuestionHighlights so the drill, blind
review, the section walk and the bootcamp capstone modal behave the same.
Blind review and the capstone modal had the tools but no handler.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" && git log --oneline -1
[/external_agent_tool_call]

## assistant

[external_agent_tool_result]
"risks": "1) Latent, pre-existing, and left alone per scope: painting splits the text nodes React rendered; when React later patches that text in place (same paragraph, different string) it updates only the first fragment and leaves the mark and tail behind, and clearMarks' normalize() then merges the garbage. I keyed the stem and choice wrappers on questionId so the question pane never takes that path, but RcPassagePane still can when a multi-passage session switches passages and both have marks in the same paragraph position. A key on the passage text div would close it; not done because it is outside the brief. 2) Click on an existing mark in a choice with no tool active both opens the Remove popover and answers with that choice (the click is collapsed and the mode is none, exactly the rule the design asked for). If that double effect is unwanted, the fix is a one-line ref set in the hook's remove rung and read in clickText. 3) React 18 event ordering: my analysis that the Remove button used to die before its click is reasoned, not observed; verify step 9 above covers it. 4) The question pane's popover dismiss runs on any mouse-up in the pane outside a surface or the popover; the passage pane's popover is separate state, so a selection in one pane leaves the other pane's popover open until its own dismiss. 5) rc_annotations rows for a passage now grow with every question's marks; there is no cap, same as before."

description: Guard clicks on marks in RC choices; typecheck
command: cd "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-fix-homework-timed-launch" && sed -n 1,30p src/features/rc/components/RcChoiceRow.tsx | grep -n "import" && python - <<'PYEOF'
p='src/features/rc/components/RcChoiceRow.tsx'
s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read()
old="""  const clickText = () => {
    if (highlightMode !== 'none') return;
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (selection && !selection.isCollapsed) return;
    pick();
  };
"""
new="""  const clickText = (event: React.MouseEvent<HTMLElement>) => {
    if (highlightMode !== 'none') return;
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    if (selection && !selection.isCollapsed) return;
    // A click on an existing mark is aimed at the mark (it opens the Remove
    // popover), not at the answer.
    if ((event.target as HTMLElement).closest?.(`[${RC_HIGHLIGHT_ATTR}]`)) return;
    pick();
  };
"""
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new,1)
if 'RC_HIGHLIGHT_ATTR' not in s.split('const clickText')[0]:
    # add import
    import re
    m=re.search(r"import \{([^}]*)\} from '../lib/rcHighlightPaint';", s)
    if m:
        s=s.replace(m.group(0), "import {"+m.group(1).rstrip()+", RC_HIGHLIGHT_ATTR } from '../lib/rcHighlightPaint';",1)
    else:
        # insert after first import line
        idx=s.index('\n', s.index('import '))+1
        s=s[:idx]+"import { RC_HIGHLIGHT_ATTR } from '../lib/rcHighlightPaint';\n"+s[idx:]
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write(s)
print('ok')

description: Run RC tests and commit
command: cd "C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/projects/lsatu-fix-homework-timed-launch" && npx vitest run src/features/rc 2>&1 | tail -4 && git add src/features/rc && git commit -q -m "RC drill: highlight and underline the stem and each answer choice

The RC player marked the passage only. The stem and the choices now
carry the same tools, the same erase, the same persistence.

Marks are stored per passage, so a stem or choice mark has to say which
question it belongs to: the target is now 'passage', 'q<id>:stem' or
'q<id>:<letter>'. Nothing had ever written a non-passage target, so
existing rows read back unchanged and no migration is needed.

The mouse-up ladder and the paint call that lived in RcPassagePane move
into useRcMarkSurface and paintRcAnnotations so the stem and five choice
rows share one implementation. Choice text leaves its button (a button
cannot be drag-selected); a plain click still answers, a drag never does,
and a click on an existing mark opens its Remove popover without
answering.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>" && git log --oneline -3
[/external_agent_tool_call]

/**
 * The current selection expressed as offsets into `container`'s rendered text.
 * Returns null when there is no selection, it is collapsed, or it falls outside.
 */
export function captureRenderedSelection(
  container: HTMLElement,
): { start: number; end: number; text: string } | null {
  const selection = window.getSelection();
  if (!selection || selection.rangeCount === 0 || selection.isCollapsed) return null;

  const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
  if (!container.contains(range.commonAncestorContainer)) return null;

  const text = selection.toString();
  if (!text.trim()) return null;

  const before = document.createRange();
  before.selectNodeContents(container);
  before.setEnd(range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
  const start = before.toString().length;

  return { start, end: start + text.length, text };
}

/** Removes marks previously painted with the given attribute, restoring the text. */
export function clearMarks(container: HTMLElement, attr: string): void {
  const marks = Array.from(container.querySelectorAll(`mark[${attr}]`));
  for (const mark of marks) {
    const parent = mark.parentNode;
    if (!parent) continue;
    while (mark.firstChild) parent.insertBefore(mark.firstChild, mark);
    parent.removeChild(mark);
  }
  container.normalize();
}

  onAnchor: (anchor: RcSelectionAnchor | null, surface: RcMarkSurfaceHandle) => void;
}

/** Rendered-text offset of the first character inside a painted mark. */
function offsetOfMark(el: HTMLElement, mark: Element): number {

  return before.toString().length;
}

/**
 * Painting highlights and find-text matches onto already-rendered markdown.
 *
 * Highlights are stored as character offsets, never as DOM nodes, so they
 * survive a reload and can be restored on any future encounter with a passage.
 * The offsets are measured against the *rendered* text of the container rather
 * than the raw markdown source: markdown syntax (the asterisks around
 * **Passage A**) never appears on screen, so an offset into the raw string does
 * not line up with anything the student can select. Rendering is deterministic
 * from the same source, so rendered offsets round-trip reliably.
 *
 * Painting walks text nodes and wraps the overlapping slice of each one, which
 * lets a highlight span element boundaries (a range crossing a bold run, say)
 * without needing the range to be well-formed for surroundContents.
 */

import { rcHighlightColor, type RcHighlight } from './rcTypes';

export const RC_HIGHLIGHT_ATTR = 'data-rc-highlight';
export const RC_FIND_ATTR = 'data-rc-find';

export interface RcTextRange {
  start: number;
  end: number;
  color?: string;
  /** 'highlight' or 'underline'; read by the caller's styleFor. */
  kind?: string;
}

/** Every text node under `root`, in document order. */
function textNodes(root: HTMLElement): Text[] {
  const walker = document.createTreeWalker(root, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
  const nodes: Text[] = [];
  let node = walker.nextNode();
  while (node) {
    nodes.push(node as Text);
    node = walker.nextNode();
  }
  return nodes;
}
 * Existing marks with the same attribute are cleared first, so this is
 * idempotent and safe to call on every render.
 */
export function paintRanges(
  container: HTMLElement,
  ranges: RcTextRange[],
  attr: string,
  styleFor?: (range: RcTextRange) => Partial<CSSStyleDeclaration>,
): void {
  clearMarks(container, attr);
  if (ranges.length === 0) return;

  for (const range of ranges) {
    if (range.end <= range.start) continue;
    // Re-walk per range: painting mutates the tree, so offsets must be
    // recomputed against the current DOM each time.
    let offset = 0;
    for (const node of textNodes(container)) {
      const length = node.data.length;
      const nodeStart = offset;
      const nodeEnd = offset + length;
      offset = nodeEnd;

      if (nodeEnd <= range.start || nodeStart >= range.end) continue;

      const from = Math.max(0, range.start - nodeStart);
      const to = Math.min(length, range.end - nodeStart);
      if (to <= from) continue;

      // Split the node so the middle piece is exactly the overlap.
      const middle = from > 0 ? node.splitText(from) : node;
      if (to - from < middle.data.length) middle.splitText(to - from);

      const mark = document.createElement('mark');
      mark.setAttribute(attr, range.color ?? 'true');
      const style = styleFor?.(range);
      if (style) Object.assign(mark.style, style);
      middle.parentNode?.insertBefore(mark, middle);
      mark.appendChild(middle);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Paints a student's stored marks onto one surface: the passage, a stem, or a
 * choice. One styling for all of them, so a mark looks the same wherever it
 * sits. Callers pass only the marks that belong to this surface.
 */
export function paintRcAnnotations(container: HTMLElement, highlights: RcHighlight[]): void {
  paintRanges(
    container,

export function captureRenderedSelection(
  container: HTMLElement,
): { start: number; end: number; text: string } | null {
  const selection = window.getSelection();
  if (!selection || selection.rangeCount === 0 || selection.isCollapsed) return null;

  const range = selection.getRangeAt(0);
  if (!container.contains(range.commonAncestorContainer)) return null;

  const text = selection.toString();
  if (!text.trim()) return null;

  const before = document.createRange();
  before.selectNodeContents(container);
  before.setEnd(range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
  const start = before.toString().length;

  return { start, end: start + text.length, text };
}'''
new='''/**
 * The current selection expressed as offsets into `container`'s rendered text.
 * Returns null when there is no selection, it is collapsed, or it never
 * touches the container.
 *
 * A selection that starts before the container or runs past it is cut at the
 * container's edges rather than rejected: a drag that overshoots the end of a
 * choice into the row's padding, or sweeps from the stem into a choice, still
 * marks the part of this surface it covered. Lengths are measured with
 * Range.toString, which concatenates text nodes exactly as painting walks
 * them; Selection.toString adds a newline at every block edge and would push
 * a multi-paragraph mark past where the drag ended.
 */
export function captureRenderedSelection(
  container: HTMLElement,
): { start: number; end: number; text: string } | null {
  const selection = window.getSelection();
  if (!selection || selection.rangeCount === 0 || selection.isCollapsed) return null;

  const raw = selection.getRangeAt(0);
  const startInside = container.contains(raw.startContainer);
  const endInside = container.contains(raw.endContainer);
  if (!startInside && !endInside) {
    const touches =
      typeof raw.intersectsNode === 'function'
        ? raw.intersectsNode(container)
        : raw.commonAncestorContainer.contains(container);
    if (!touches) return null;
  }

  const range = raw.cloneRange();
  if (!startInside) range.setStart(container, 0);
  if (!endInside) range.setEnd(container, container.childNodes.length);
  if (range.collapsed) return null;

  const text = range.toString();
  if (!text.trim()) return null;

  const before = document.createRange();
  before.selectNodeContents(container);
  before.setEnd(range.startContainer, range.startOffset);
  const start = before.toString().length;

  return { start, end: start + text.length, text };
}'''

  // marks. The store hands over one array per passage, so a mark on a stem or
  // a choice makes a new array too; without this the passage would be
  // repainted (and the find marks with it) for every mark made anywhere.
  const filtered = React.useMemo(
    () => highlights.filter((h) => h.target === 'passage'),
    [highlights],
  );
  const stable = React.useRef(filtered);
  if (
    stable.current !== filtered &&
    (stable.current.length !== filtered.length ||
      stable.current.some((h, i) => h !== filtered[i]))
  ) {
    stable.current = filtered;
  }
  const passageHighlights = stable.current;
'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new,1)
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write(s)
print('ok')

119-  it('the letter badge still answers', () => {
import * as React from 'react';
import { cleanup, fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { RcChoiceRow } from './RcChoiceRow';
import type { RcChoice, RcHighlight, RcHighlightMode } from '../lib/rcTypes';

const choice: RcChoice = {
  letter: 'B',
  text: 'The author regards the theory as incomplete.',
  tag: null,
  percent: null,
  responseCount: null,
  correct: false,
  explanation: null,
};

interface Overrides {
  highlightMode?: RcHighlightMode;
  eliminated?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  revealed?: boolean;
  highlights?: RcHighlight[];
}

function renderRow(overrides: Overrides = {}) {
  const onSelect = vi.fn();
  const onAnchor = vi.fn();
  const paneRef = React.createRef<HTMLUListElement>();
  render(
    <ul ref={paneRef}>
      <RcChoiceRow
        choice={choice}
        questionId={42}
        selected={false}
        eliminated={overrides.eliminated ?? false}
        revealed={overrides.revealed ?? false}
        disabled={overrides.disabled}
        onSelect={onSelect}
        onToggleElimination={vi.fn()}
        highlights={overrides.highlights ?? []}
        color="yellow"
        highlightMode={overrides.highlightMode ?? 'none'}
        onAddHighlight={vi.fn()}
        onRemoveHighlight={vi.fn()}
        onRemoveRange={vi.fn()}
        onAnchor={onAnchor}
        paneRef={paneRef}
      />
    </ul>,
  );
  const text = screen.getByText(/regards the theory/);
  return { onSelect, onAnchor, text };
}

/** A drag across the choice text, as the browser leaves it behind on click. */
function selectAll(el: HTMLElement) {
  const range = document.createRange();
  range.selectNodeContents(el);
  const selection = window.getSelection()!;
  selection.removeAllRanges();
[/external_agent_tool_result]

    for (const mode of ['yellow', 'pink', 'orange', 'underline', 'erase'] as const) {

      fireEvent.mouseUp(text);

  });
'''
new='''  it('under a marker a plain click still answers, after a beat', () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    try {
      for (const mode of ['yellow', 'pink', 'orange', 'underline'] as const) {
        const { onSelect, text } = renderRow({ highlightMode: mode });
        fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
        fireEvent.click(text);
        expect(onSelect, mode).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(350);
        expect(onSelect, mode).toHaveBeenCalledWith('B');
        cleanup();
      }
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers();
    }
  });

  it('with the eraser a click beside a mark still answers', () => {
    const { onSelect, text } = renderRow({ highlightMode: 'erase' });
    fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
    fireEvent.click(text);
    expect(onSelect).toHaveBeenCalledWith('B');
  });

  it('under a marker a double-click on a word marks it and never answers', () => {
    // Browser order: down, up, click (caret only), down (word selected), up,
    // click, dblclick. The first click cannot yet tell it is not a pick.
    vi.useFakeTimers();
    try {
      const { onSelect, text } = renderRow({ highlightMode: 'yellow' });
      fireEvent.mouseDown(text);
      fireEvent.mouseUp(text);
      fireEvent.click(text, { detail: 1 });
      fireEvent.mouseDown(text, { detail: 2 });
      selectAll(text);
      fireEvent.mouseUp(text, { detail: 2 });
      fireEvent.click(text, { detail: 2 });
      fireEvent.doubleClick(text);
      vi.advanceTimersByTime(500);
      expect(onSelect).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    } finally {
      vi.useRealTimers();
    }
  });

  it('a mark is taken even when the mouse is released outside the text', () => {
    // The drag ran past the end of the choice into the row's padding.
    const onAdd = vi.fn();
    const paneRef = React.createRef<HTMLUListElement>();
    render(
      <ul ref={paneRef}>
        <RcChoiceRow
          choice={choice}
          questionId={42}
          selected={false}
          eliminated={false}
          revealed={false}
          onSelect={vi.fn()}
          onToggleElimination={vi.fn()}
          highlights={[]}
          color="yellow"
          highlightMode="pink"
          onAddHighlight={onAdd}
          onRemoveHighlight={vi.fn()}
          onRemoveRange={vi.fn()}
          onAnchor={vi.fn()}
          paneRef={paneRef}
        />
      </ul>,
    );
    const text = screen.getByText(/regards the theory/);
    selectAll(text);
    fireEvent.mouseUp(paneRef.current!);
    expect(onAdd).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringMatching(/^q42:B$/), 0, choice.text.length, 'pink', 'highlight');
  });
'''
assert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new,1)
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8',newline='\n').write(s)
print('ok')

## assistant

Done. Highlighting and underlining now work on the question stem and every answer choice, in every place a student drills, and it's pushed to PR #121 (now 8 commits).

## What was actually wrong

Three things, not one:

1. **Choices were `<button>`s.** A browser won't start a drag-selection inside a button, so the marker could never touch them. On every LR surface.
2. **A hidden bug was killing stem highlights.** Overlap between highlights was tested by character offset with no section check, so highlighting the opening of the stimulus silently deleted a stem highlight at the same offsets. That's why the stem felt broken even though it was wired.
3. **RC had no stem or choice surface at all**, and its store couldn't say which question a mark belonged to.

## What shipped

**LR** (drill, blind review, section walk, bootcamp capstone modal, all through one shared hook so they behave identically):
- Choice text is a plain block: a click still picks the answer, a drag never does, the letter badge stays the keyboard control.
- Each section (stimulus, stem, choice A..E) has its own lane, so marks never fight each other.
- One drag from the stem into two choices marks all three. A drag that starts on the question number snaps to the stem instead of shifting by four characters.
- Blind review and the capstone modal had the tools but dead handlers; both work now.

**RC:** stem and each choice take highlight/underline/erase with the same tools as the passage, and marks persist (scoped as `q<id>:stem` / `q<id>:A`, no migration needed).

## Review pass

I ran an adversarial review over the diff (5 lenses, every finding independently verified). Nine were real; all fixed and re-verified in the browser:
- Under a marker tool, **double-clicking a word in a choice also picked it** (first click lands before the word is selected). A pick from the text now waits 300ms while a marker is on; immediate with no tool.
- RC dropped a mark if the mouse was released in the row padding; the RC Highlight popover survived Next and could write old offsets onto the new question; the capstone modal's Directions dialog opened under the modal and Escape closed the modal from the Find box.

Five findings were refuted as pre-existing (e.g. multiply-blend marks invisible on the black/navy RC colour schemes, which was already true for passage marks).

## Verified live on the dev build

LR section, LR blind review, and RC drill: stimulus + stem + choice marks coexisting, plain click picks, drag doesn't, double-click marks only, eraser click/drag, release-outside, cross-block drag, popover reset on navigation, RC marks surviving reload.

**Tests:** 410 pass; 4 fail in `SingleSession.test.tsx` (booking slot "1:00 PM"), which fail identically on the main checkout on a different branch, so pre-existing and clock-dependent. Typecheck clean.

## Skipped, and why

- **Classic layout** (`?layout=classic`, not reachable by students without the URL param): still stimulus + stem only.
- **Causation Station** bootcamp cards use their own one-colour model; not touched.
- **LRQuestionModal** (bootcamp capstone) was verified by tests and the reviewers, not by me in the browser; I didn't have a capstone open.

Same standing questions as before: merge + publish #121, and Codex review first or not. Avneet's PT156.S2 assignment (due 2026-09-12) is still waiting on that.

      1 53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76
---
name: lovable-frontend-publish-is-separate
description: A push builds a Lovable preview but never reaches lsatprep.study; publish with deploy_project, wait for the sync, and prove it from the bundle.
metadata:
  type: project
---

Pushing to `main` makes Lovable build the commit — its project record shows a
preview screenshot tagged with the commit sha within minutes — but the published
site at lsatprep.study keeps serving the previous bundle until something calls
`deploy_project` (Lovable MCP, `project_id 53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76`,
name `lsatu`). Confirmed 2026-09-08: a fix sat built-but-unpublished for 20
minutes with no error anywhere.

**The sync lags the push.** `deploy_project` publishes whatever Lovable has
ingested, not what is on origin. On 2026-09-09 a deploy fired seconds after a
successful push and shipped the *previous* commit; `get_project` showed
`latest_commit_sha` catching up about three minutes later, and a second deploy
was needed. There is no error and no warning — the deploy reports `pending` and
succeeds, just on the wrong commit.

**Why:** build, sync and publish are three separate steps, and none of them
reports that the others have not happened. A green push reads as shipped.

**How to apply:** before calling `deploy_project`, call `get_project` and check
`latest_commit_sha` equals your HEAD; if it does not, wait and re-check. After
deploying, prove it from the bundle: pick a string **the commit alone**
introduces — not a class name an earlier deploy already shipped, which is how a
stale bundle passed a check on 2026-09-09 — then fetch `https://lsatprep.study/`,
read the `index-*` asset hash out of it, and grep the asset for the marker.
Allow several minutes; the hash changing is the signal a publish landed at all.
Note `deploy_project` ships every unpublished commit on main, not just yours.

Same trap, different half: [[lovable-edge-functions-do-not-deploy-from-push]].
Related: [[push-only-your-own-commits]].
[/external_agent_tool_result]

Everything is live and proven from the served bundles. Here is exactly what was published.

## What went to main and is now live on lsatprep.study

**Merge:** PR #121 → `main` as `0d77df6`, merged 2026-09-11 09:41 UTC. Main had not moved since the branch was cut, so the merge is exactly these 8 commits and nothing else rode along.

**Publish:** Lovable `deploy_project` → live bundle changed from `index-C0EdWv-I.js` to `index-Cy8nC5rk.js`. The first deploy call did nothing visible after 22 minutes; the second one landed ~9 minutes later. I then fetched the live bundle, its 437 lazy chunks and the CSS chunk, and grepped for strings that exist only in these commits. All present.

### 1. Section directions (the LawHub-style opening)
- Opening an assigned PT section lands on the directions page: Section N, Total Section Time, question count, official directions, GO TO QUESTIONS. Clock starts on GO TO QUESTIONS, not before. Back to home link, no floating help button.
- Inside the section, the **Directions** button swaps the page to the directions card with the clock still running top-right and a **Questions** button top-left. Verified live on dev: clock ran continuously through three round trips.
- The card is one shared component for both, with the big bold "Section 2" title.

### 2. Pause and section behaviour
- LawHub "Notice" pause dialog on every full section and PT sitting, with Cancel / Ok / "Don't show me this message again" (per sitting).
- Assigned sections are sealed by default (no Answer/Explanation mid-section), "Time's up." → Go to blind review, blind review auto-opens after an assigned section, completion self-closes.
- Not exercised live by me: the 35-minute Time's-up path and auto blind review. Code paths and tests only.

### 3. Highlight and underline on stem and answer choices
- **LR** (drill, blind review, section review walk, bootcamp capstone modal): all three marker colours and underline on stimulus, stem, and each choice; eraser click and drag; one drag can span stem into choices; a plain click still picks the answer, a drag never does, double-click on a word marks it without picking. Also fixed the hidden bug where a stimulus highlight silently deleted a stem highlight.
- **RC:** stem and each choice take the same tools as the passage, persist across reload, marks land even if the mouse is released in the row padding, and the Highlight popover no longer survives into the next question.

### Verified how
- Live on the dev build in the Browser pane: LR section, LR blind review, RC drill, all the interactions above.
- Tests: 410 pass. 4 fail in `SingleSession.test.tsx` (booking page) and fail identically on the main checkout on another branch; pre-existing, clock-dependent, unrelated.
- Adversarial review: 9 confirmed findings, all fixed and re-verified before merge.
- Production: bundle hash changed, and every commit-specific marker (`GO TO QUESTIONS`, the pause notice text, `section-directions-questions` CSS, `data-rc-mark-surface`, `consumedLastMouseUp` from the final commit) is in the served assets.

**Not published / not done:** the RC choice highlighter was not eyeballed on production itself, only on dev plus bundle proof. Avneet's PT156.S2 assignment (due 2026-09-12) is still not sent; say the word and I'll run it from the assign skill now that this is live.

# Session Handoff — PR #121 shipped to lsatprep.study: LawHub directions screen, pause notice, stem + answer-choice highlighting (LR and RC)

## Where it started
Picked up from a handoff where PR #121 (LawHub directions page for assigned sections) was open on `feat/section-directions-card`, awaiting Joshua's review. Joshua then asked for two things this session: (1) the in-section Directions button should turn the page into the directions screen with the clock still running, matching a LawHub screenshot he pasted; (2) highlight and underline must work on the question stem and every answer choice on all drilling surfaces, not just the stimulus. He then said "no Codex review, merge and push to main, publish, make it live, be clear about what you're publishing." Ultracode was on for the highlighting work, so it ran as workflows (understand → implement RC in parallel with my inline LR work → adversarial review).

## Decisions locked + what shipped
- Directions mid-section = full-page swap, not a dialog: `Questions` button top-left, `TIME REMAINING` clock top-right (never paused), card, `GO TO QUESTIONS`. Only for full sections and sittings; other drills keep the dialog. Card extracted to `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os\projects\lsatu-fix-homework-timed-launch\src\components\drill\official\SectionDirectionsCard.tsx`; title now bold 34px on both the pre-section page and the in-section view. Toolbar gained `onOpenDirections`. `directionsViewOpen` state in `Drill.tsx` resets on run change.
- Root cause of "stem highlighting doesn't work" was a shared-core bug: `replaceOverlappingHighlights` compared offsets with no section guard, so a stimulus highlight deleted a stem highlight at the same offsets. Fixed; every section is its own lane.
- LR highlight model widened: `HighlightSection = 'stimulus' | 'stem' | \`choice:${key}\`` in `src\lib\highlightUtils.ts`, with clamped multi-region capture (`captureSelectionAcross`), snap-to-segment for drags starting on the question number, and `eraseRange`. New shared hook `src\hooks\useQuestionHighlights.ts` used by `Drill.tsx`, `EnhancedBlindReview.tsx`, `SectionReviewWalk.tsx` (now annotatable; record still inert), `LRQuestionModal.tsx` (gained the toolbar with `directions={null}`).
- `OfficialQuestionPane.tsx`: choice text left its `<button>` (browsers will not drag-select inside one); one root `onMouseUp` hands all regions to the handler; plain click picks, drag never does; under a marker tool a pick from text waits 300ms (`src\hooks\useDeferredPick.ts`) so double-click-to-mark-a-word does not pick; eraser click on a highlight erases, beside one picks; badge button is the keyboard control with `aria-describedby`.
- `onTextSelection` prop signature changed to `(regions: HighlightRegion[]) => boolean | void`. Legacy `BlindReviewFlow.tsx` untouched (effectively unreachable). Classic layout (`?layout=classic`) only re-wired for stimulus/stem, no choices.
- RC: target model is now `'passage' | \`q${id}:stem\` | \`q${id}:${letter}\`` with `questionTarget()` in `src\features\rc\lib\rcTypes.ts`; no migration (jsonb). Mouse-up ladder extracted to `src\features\rc\hooks\useRcMarkSurface.ts`, which listens on `document` and clips the selection to its surface (marks land when released in padding or across surfaces; a release with no selection dismisses every popover). Paint helper `paintRcAnnotations` in `rcHighlightPaint.ts`; `captureRenderedSelection` now clamps and uses `Range.toString` (was `Selection.toString`, which over-counted paragraph breaks). `RcQuestionPane` popover holds a list of surfaces (Highlight paints all touched), resets on question change; `RcChoiceRow` keyed on `questionId:letter`, text out of button, same deferred-pick rules as LR. `RcPassagePane` keeps a stable filtered array so choice marks do not repaint the passage.
- Adversarial review (5 lenses, 24 agents): 9 confirmed findings all fixed in `0815282`; 5 refuted as pre-existing (multiply-blend marks invisible on black/navy RC schemes; unkeyed passage text node splitting; BlindReviewFlow old model; missing hook tests; etc.).
- PR #121 merged into `main` as `0d77df6` at 09:41 UTC 2026-09-11 (8 commits: `2bf8f3b b0b4871 b1d2347 c89bb91 615500b 78bcdf8 de95f99 0815282`). Published via Lovable `deploy_project` (project `53b9a6f2-aba7-4ae4-8dd1-fa5b8aab4d76`): first deploy stalled 22 min with no change, second deploy landed ~9 min later. Live bundle `index-C0EdWv-I.js` → `index-Cy8nC5rk.js`.
- A Lovable bot commit `9b6b51c "Fixed production build error"` (10:10 UTC, co-authored by Joshua's account, from the Lovable editor) sits on main after my merge; it only restores 589 lines of `supabase/functions/mcp/index.ts`. Not my change; edge function only; likely why the first deploy stalled. Edge functions do not deploy from push (see memory), so that function may still need its own deploy if it matters.
- No memory files written this session. No vault files written.
